    src = src.replace(old, new)

p = sys.argv[1]
src = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
def rep(old, new):
    global src
    n = src.count(old)
    assert n == 1, (n, old[:90])
    src = src.replace(old, new)

rep('EXPECTED_CHECKS = 444        #', 'EXPECTED_CHECKS = 445        #')
rep('''              "C3-ENDLOOK-REUSE-GUARDS", "C3-ENDLOOK-STOP-CLASS"]
''', '''              "C3-ENDLOOK-REUSE-GUARDS", "C3-ENDLOOK-STOP-CLASS"]
_REQUIRED += ["C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY"]                         # the seat's step-15 answers, Q2 (PIN-45's class check)
''')
# the existing class check: its label scoped to the fired end looks
rep('''            B.chk("refused at V2 (PIN-45, the end's class at a STOP whose end look fires, checked by ids against the "''',
    '''            B.chk("refused at V2 (PIN-45, the end's class at a STOP whose end look fires -- at a silent one and with "
                  "none, C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY -- checked by ids against the "''')
# the new check, after the existing one
rep('''                  cover=["C3-ENDLOOK-STOP-CLASS"])
''', '''                  cover=["C3-ENDLOOK-STOP-CLASS"])
            # the end's class at EVERY STOP (PIN-45; the seat's step-15 answers, Q2): at a silent end look, fresh or
            # reused, and with no end look, where no pause rule runs -- each one field (or one id) edited, offered
            # with the seat inputs the edited class asks for; unedited, each is analyzed

            MSG_H = "REFUSED: a hair-trigger STOP's session ("

                        v2_edit(cp_h, "every-h3-tb", stop_set(bar="template_breakage")), MSG_G),

            k_ev0 = {"hair 3": v2_edit(cp_h, "every-h3-0", lambda ck_: None, pis_=True),

                     "G-VER 1": v2_edit(cp_g0e, "every-g0-0", lambda ck_: None)}
            el_h_, el_p5_, el_g1_ = (c_["campaign"]["end_look"] or {} for c_ in (ck_h, ck_p5e, ck_g1e))
            B.chk("refused at V2 (PIN-45, the end's class at every STOP, the seat's step-15 answers (Q2), checked by ids "
                  "against the writer's assertions in stages._stop_end_look, which it makes at every STOP before its "

---- Code's probe of the class check and the stop->terminal route (mkprobe_r1.py), verbatim
import sys
src = open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8").read()
anchor = '            B.chk("refused at V2 (PIN-45, the end\'s class at a STOP whose end look fires, checked by ids against the "'
assert src.count(anchor) == 1
probe = '''            # PROBE (Code, step-15b r1): the class check at every STOP, and the stop->terminal route's scope
            def p_relabel(bar_, sid_=None):
                def f_(ck_):
                    ck_["stop"]["bar"] = bar_
                    if sid_ is not None:
                        ck_["stop"].update(template=sid_[0], s=sid_[1])
                return f_

            def p_term(st_, rule_=False, floors0_=False):
                def f_(ck_):
                    ck_.pop("stop")
                    ck_["campaign"]["terminal"] = {"status": st_, "at_launches": len(ck_["sessions"])}
                    if st_ == "PI-STOP":
                        ck_["campaign"]["pi_stop"] = "hand: a PI stop (probe)"
                    if rule_:
                        ck_["campaign"]["pauses"][-1]["adjudication"] = "hand: a ruling (probe)"
                    if floors0_:
                        ck_["campaign"]["floors"] = {k_: 0 for k_ in ck_["campaign"]["floors"]}
                return f_

            def p_gver_moved(rule_=False):
                def f_(ck_):
                    ck_["stop"].update(bar="template_breakage", template="A0", s=999)
                    if rule_:
                        ck_["campaign"]["pauses"][-1]["adjudication"] = "hand: a ruling (probe)"
                return f_
            cp_g1_ = Path(res_g1["checkpoint"])
            cp_g0_ = Path(res_g0["checkpoint"])
            cp_p5_ = prod["P5"][0]
            hfz_ = {"kind": "end_look", "at_launches": json.loads(cp_hf.read_text())["campaign"]["pauses"][-1]["at_launches"]}
            probes_ = {
                "A1 h3 silent fresh -> template_breakage (one field), no seat input": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-a1", p_relabel("template_breakage")),
                "A2 P5 silent reused -> schedule_mismatch, no seat input": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-a2", p_relabel("schedule_mismatch")),
                "A3 g1 G-VER silent fresh -> hair bar, PI-stop record": v2_edit(cp_g1_, "pb-a3", p_relabel(HAIR_BAR), pis_=True),
                "A4 g0 G-VER no session -> hair bar, PI-stop record": v2_edit(cp_g0_, "pb-a4", p_relabel(HAIR_BAR), pis_=True),
                "A5 g1 G-VER silent, id set to the last session's": v2_edit(cp_g1_, "pb-a5", lambda ck_: ck_["stop"].update(
                    template=ck_["sessions"][-1]["template"], s=ck_["sessions"][-1]["s"])),
                "A6 h3 unedited, PI-stop record": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-a6", lambda ck_: None, pis_=True),
                "A7 g1 unedited, no seat input": v2_edit(cp_g1_, "pb-a7", lambda ck_: None),
                "A8 g0 unedited, no seat input": v2_edit(cp_g0_, "pb-a8", lambda ck_: None),
                "A9 P5 unedited, PI-stop record": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-a9", lambda ck_: None, pis_=True),
                "T1 h3 silent fresh -> PI-STOP": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-t1", p_term("PI-STOP")),
                "T2 h3 silent fresh -> CAP": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-t2", p_term("CAP")),
                "T3 h3 silent fresh -> COMPLETE": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-t3", p_term("COMPLETE")),
                "T4 h3 silent fresh -> COMPLETE, floors 0": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-t4", p_term("COMPLETE", floors0_=True)),
                "T5 hf fired fresh -> PI-STOP, adjudication": v2_edit(cp_hf, "pb-t5", p_term("PI-STOP"), adj_pause=hfz_),
                "T6 hf fired fresh -> CAP, adjudication": v2_edit(cp_hf, "pb-t6", p_term("CAP"), adj_pause=hfz_),
                "T7 hf fired fresh -> PI-STOP, no seat input": v2_edit(cp_hf, "pb-t7", p_term("PI-STOP")),
                "T8 hl fired reused -> PI-STOP, pause ruled": v2_edit(cp_hl, "pb-t8", p_term("PI-STOP", rule_=True)),
                "T9 hl fired reused -> CAP, pause ruled": v2_edit(cp_hl, "pb-t9", p_term("CAP", rule_=True)),
                "T10 hl fired reused -> PI-STOP, pause unruled, adjudication": v2_edit(cp_hl, "pb-t10", p_term("PI-STOP"), adj_pause=hl_pz),
                "T11 P5 silent reused -> PI-STOP": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-t11", p_term("PI-STOP")),
                "T12 P5 silent reused -> CAP": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-t12", p_term("CAP")),
                "T13 P5 silent reused -> COMPLETE": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-t13", p_term("COMPLETE")),
                "G1 h3 silent -> G-VER, id moved off the record": v2_edit(cp_h, "pb-g1", p_gver_moved()),
                "G2 hf fired fresh -> G-VER, id moved, adjudication": v2_edit(cp_hf, "pb-g2", p_gver_moved(), adj_pause=hfz_),
                "G3 hl fired reused -> G-VER, id moved, pause ruled": v2_edit(cp_hl, "pb-g3", p_gver_moved(rule_=True)),
                "G4 P5 silent reused -> G-VER, id moved": v2_edit(cp_p5_, "pb-g4", p_gver_moved()),

            for k_, r_ in probes_.items():
                print("PROBE-R1", k_, "|", r_[0][0], "|", r_[0][1][:170], "| release calls", r_[1], "| written", r_[2], flush=True)
'''
src = src.replace(anchor, probe + anchor)
open(sys.argv[2], "w", encoding="utf-8").write(src)
print("ok")

---- THE WORKFLOW SCRIPT (step15b-r1-review-wf_0121cd84-0a8.js), verbatim
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-r1-review',
  description: 'Review the step-15 second analyzer round (r1): fidelity, refusal probes, battery and mutation, each with a strict refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Review' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}

const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'

const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. You review Code's second analyzer round of PROPOSAL-m step 15 (repo ${R}). The build under review is
${S2}/build/m_analyze.r1.py; the committed analyzer at HEAD is ${S2}/build/m_analyze.base.py (sha256 522e8057...,
= scripts/m_analyze.py at HEAD); the unified diff is ${S2}/build/r1.diff.

WHAT THE ROUND IS ORDERED TO DO (the seat's step-15 answers, ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md --
read it in full first): (i) Q2: PIN-45's ids-only class check runs at every STOP, not only at a STOP whose end look fired;
(ii) Q5: PIN-9's and PIN-10's "flagged for the seat" over the pause raised at a STOP and its route become "the seat's by
R2 2 (2026-10-10)"; PIN-7's list of V2 structural refusals and PIN-10's statement of the end-look refusal name PIN-45 and
PIN-46; (iii) PIN-45's text states the stop->terminal route at its full scope (at a silent look, at a fresh fired look
given the adjudication, and at a reused look). Nothing else enters the analyzer this round except, later, PIN-17's anchor
(after the PI places preprint patch v8) and any KINDS_VOCAB/schema need from the sentence table's draft -- NOT in r1.
Q1: PIN-44/45/46 stay Code's readings; (c)'s widening is accepted, "PIN-46 says so; nothing is struck" (PIN-46 unchanged).
Code's own additions in r1, to be judged on their merits: PIN-45's limit sentence names the terminal statuses (PI-STOP or
CAP; COMPLETE only where every arm reaches its recorded floor, else V4 STOPs) from Code's probe; PIN-10's V2 site comment
gains one sentence naming PIN-46/PIN-45; the battery gains check C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY (445 checks, 458 coverage ids) and
STR-PIN-V10-TEXTS gains a fourth seat form ('the seat's step-15 answers ('), a seat-source copy of Q2's first paragraph,
PIN-45's required quote of Q2's order, and three plants.
Other sources: R2 = ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1_round2.md (item 2 made the pause at a STOP,
its route and the V2 refusal the seat's); Code's ping = ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-code-to-design_step15.md (Q2, Q5);
last round's review raw = ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_review.txt (R3M-5 near lines 4820-4840 and
6535-6560). The writer: ${R}/valence_core/m/stages.py (_stop_end_look ~2273-2325, the PI-stop/resume path ~2085-2140),
${R}/valence_core/m/resume.py (ended_status, _check_state).

HOW TO RUN THE BATTERY. ${S2}/runst15b.sh <src.py> <run-id> [${S2}/mut15.py <mutant-id>] clones HEAD into a scratch dir
(the clone's data/m/ckpt/*.json removed unread), copies <src.py> over scripts/m_analyze.py, optionally applies ONE mutant
(mut15.py reads the JSON list named by env MUTANTS: [{"id","old","new"[, "count"]}] or [{"id","edits":[[old,new],...]}];
'old' must occur exactly once (or 'count' times)), runs the battery (~25 s; model imports and sockets blocked) and writes
<OUTDIR>/<run-id>.txt (+ .diff). ALWAYS set your own env OUTDIR and WDIR under ${S2}/<your-dir>/ so you never collide
with other reviewers or the background batch (which uses ${S2}/prev_r1 and ${S2}/w_prev_r1 -- do not touch those).
For probes, copy r1 to a new file under your dir, add PRINT-ONLY blocks inside the battery (e.g. before the B.chk of
C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY, where fixtures cp_h, cp_hf, cp_hl, cp_ge, prod["P1".."P10"], res_g0, res_g1, v2_edit, stop_set,
stop_on, hl_pz, HAIR_BAR, GVER_BARS are in scope; v2_edit(src_path, name, fn, adj_pause=..., pis_=True) returns
((status, message), release_calls, written_files)), and run it through runst15b.sh. Use the venv python
${R}/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python with -I for any script.

HARD RULES. Never open, list or hash anything under data/m/ckpt/ (in the repo or any clone), and never open
results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt or data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json. Never run git inside jlens-qwen36/.
Do not modify anything in ${R} (scratch only), do not commit. Do not read or touch scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*,
README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg. Report only what you verified; cite file:line in r1
and probe output paths. Severity: blocker (wrong behaviour or a false text of record), minor (imprecise text, missing
case), note (observation for the record).`

const FINDINGS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    summary: { type: 'string' },
    findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['blocker', 'minor', 'note'] },
      where: { type: 'string' }, finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
    verified_ok: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
    mutants: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, intent: { type: 'string' }, outcome: { type: 'string' }, detail: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'outcome'] } },
  },
  required: ['summary', 'findings', 'verified_ok'],
}
const VERDICTS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] },
      evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
    missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
  },
  required: ['verdicts', 'missed'],
}

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', dir: 'lens_fid', prompt: `LENS: FIDELITY AND TEXTS. Work dir ${S2}/lens_fid.
(1) Is each ordered change made, and nothing beyond the order and Code's declared additions? Walk every hunk of r1.diff.
(2) Does every new or changed text say exactly what the code does: PIN-45's register text and its site comment (the
marker block before the class check), PIN-7, PIN-9 (register and site comment), PIN-10 (register and V2 site comment),
the battery labels of C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY, C3-ENDLOOK-STOP-CLASS and STR-PIN-V10-TEXTS, the comments near the moved code.
(3) Every quotation of the seat verbatim in its source (markdown emphasis removed); dates, ruling ids and question numbers
right; the battery's seat-source copy of Q2 verbatim against the answers file.
(4) Search ALL of r1 (module docstring, every PIN text, SEAT_ROUTED, comments, labels) for text this round makes stale or
leaves stale: anything saying the class check runs only at a fired end look; anything still crediting the pause at a STOP
or its route to Code 'flagged for the seat' (note: the answers say stages.py's parallel text stays unamended, and the
package is not in scope); counts 444/457 where 445/458 is now true.
(5) PIN-45's new limit sentence: is each clause true and is the full scope stated (verify by your own probe runs: hair
STOPs relabelled to terminal PI-STOP / CAP / COMPLETE at a silent fresh, a fired fresh (with and without the
adjudication), a silent reused and a fired reused end look (with and without a ruling)). Is 'with the shape of that end at
n, which ids cannot tell apart' true for CAP (could the writer end CAP at any n?) and for PI-STOP at a silent fresh look
(could the writer PI-stop a running, unpaused campaign at any n? read stages.py ~2085-2140)?
(6) Anything in r1 that conflicts with Q1 (PIN-46 unchanged; nothing struck).` },
  { key: 'refusals', dir: 'lens_ref', prompt: `LENS: REFUSALS, PROBED. Work dir ${S2}/lens_ref.
Adversarially probe r1's V2 refusals with print-only probe copies run through runst15b.sh.
(1) The class check at every STOP: hand edits of the stop block -- bar, template, s; s as float / bool / str; template
not a str; keys missing; extra keys; stop not an object (list, string, number, empty dict -- note resume.ended_status
reads ck.get("stop") truthiness) -- at each end shape you can build from the fixtures (hair and G-VER x silent fresh,
silent reused, fired fresh, fired reused, no end look / no session). Which are refused, with which message, and which
reach VERDICTS? Any traceback (a crash instead of a refusal) is a blocker.
(2) No false refusal: every production-written end the battery builds is accepted unedited with its due seat inputs
(P1-P10 and the hair/G-VER fixtures). Read stages.py's STOP writers: can the writer ever write a STOP whose ids this check
refuses (e.g. a hair trigger whose session is not the last recorded, a G-VER STOP whose id equals a recorded session's)?
(3) Refusal order and messages: PIN-10 -> PIN-46 -> the class check -> PIN-45's pause rule -> PIN-9 -> PIN-8 -> other STOP
bar. Build cases combining two defects and confirm which message wins and that it matches the texts (PIN-45 says the
class check is 'after PIN-46's and before PIN-9's branch'). Exit status 2, release_full not called, nothing written.
(4) PIN-45's limit sentence, empirically: stop->terminal (PI-STOP, CAP, COMPLETE) at each look shape and seat-input
combination; the G-VER relabel with the id moved off the record and to the arm's next launch. Is any route the text
states open in fact closed, or any open route unstated?` },
  { key: 'battery', dir: 'lens_bat', prompt: `LENS: BATTERY AND MUTATION. Work dir ${S2}/lens_bat.
(1) Do C3-STOP-CLASS-EVERY's cases and the STR-PIN-V10-TEXTS additions mean what their labels say (fixtures as described,
each refusal by its own message, unedited accepted, each plant caught by its own problem)?
(2) Write mutants against r1's changed code and battery -- the class-check block (its guard isinstance(stop_el, Mapping),
its placement relative to PIN-46, PIN-45's pause rule and PIN-9, each component of the hair and G-VER rules), the
end_raised computation that now relies on the outer stop_el, v10_problems' new form, source, quote and plants,
EXPECTED_CHECKS and _REQUIRED -- in mut15.py's JSON form, in a file under your dir (set MUTANTS to it). Run each through
runst15b.sh (6 in parallel at most). Report each: KILLED (by which check), SURVIVED, or EQUIVALENT (with the reason).
(3) Last round's mutants that no longer apply to r1 as written are in ${S2}/mutants_prev_reexpress.json (26; most target
the class check, which moved out of the fired branch, or text this round changed). Re-express each against r1 preserving
its intent (or say why its intent no longer exists in r1), run it, report it under its original id with suffix ':re-b1'.
(4) For every non-equivalent survivor, propose the battery case that would kill it (exact code).` },
]

const results = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + '\n\n' + l.prompt, { label: 'lens:' + l.key, phase: 'Review', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens. Work dir ${S2}/ref_${l.key}.
Its report (verbatim JSON):\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\n
For EACH finding: reproduce it independently (rerun probes or mutants yourself, re-read the code and sources); try hard to
refute it; give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your own evidence (paths) and, if confirmed, the minimal fix (exact
text or code). Default to REFUTED when you cannot reproduce. Also re-run 3 of its 'verified_ok' claims and 3 of its
KILLED/EQUIVALENT mutant outcomes (if any) and report any that do not hold as an entry in 'missed', together with
anything the lens should have found and did not.`, { label: 'refute:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
    .then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null,
)
return results

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF ROUND 1 (disp_r1.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of review round 1 (r1, sha256 d7d5f7fa…) — applied as r2 unless stated

The lenses reviewed r1.py itself; build/r1.diff predated r1's last edit by one sentence (FID-5, REF-6, LB-6), and every
round from here gets its diff cut from the file it reviews.

## Applied in r2

- **FID-1** (refuter: PARTLY — substance confirmed, blocker grade not accepted, since "which ids cannot tell apart" is
  literally true). PIN-45's stop->terminal limit is rewritten so that PI-STOP and CAP are stated apart: as a PI-STOP the
  rewrite has the ids of the PI's stop at n, which the writer can make at any n ≥ 1, and the fields that differ
  (campaign.pi_stop, the stop invocation's provenance entry) V2 does not read (**FID-4**); as a CAP it passes at any n,
  as V2 reads no hour cap (the writer ends CAP only where resume.hour_cap_exceeded holds); COMPLETE as before. The stop
  block "removed (or left falsy, as resume.ended_status reads its truthiness)" (**REF-4(a)**).
  Not built: a V2 read of the hour cap. It would be a new refusal beyond the writer's assertions, and the seat's Q2 says
  "Nothing else enters the analyzer". It is a declared limit in PIN-45's text and goes to v1.0's list. If it is ever
  built, the battery's cap fixtures (P10 and the cap-patched runs) must first be re-made with a real cap (the fidelity
  refuter's note).
- **FID-2, REF-5, LB-6 (i), and the refusals refuter's note on the register sentence.** PIN-10's site sentence and
  register sentence now read: "V2 also checks a recorded end look's launch count (PIN-46), a STOP's class by ids and a
  fired end look's matching pause (PIN-45)" (the site's begins "Next,").
- **REF-2.** The "refused after PIN-8 whichever rule is chosen" texts (PIN-45's register text, its site comment, the
  inner comment, the pause rule's comment) now say: any other STOP bar (a truthy stop block that is not an object
  included) is refused after PIN-8, or earlier by the end-raised pause rule it selects, where the end look reuses a
  fired look whose pause was ruled before the end. The behaviour is unchanged (a refusal either way). Moving the
  refusal to the class check would be beyond the order and is not done. C3-STOP-CLASS-OTHER-BAR pins both halves.
- **FID-8.** The new check's label says the unedited G-VER STOP at launch 1 is analyzed as a NOT-RUN; the seat-source
  copy is named "its first paragraph" of Q2 in the key, the comment and the label.
- **REF-1, LB-1.** New check C3-STOP-CLASS-ORDER: five two-defect cases pin the class check after PIN-10's C9(ii)
  refusal and PIN-46's, before PIN-45's pause rule and PIN-9's and PIN-8's branches.
- **LB-2.** New check C3-STOP-CLASS-OTHER-BAR: an object stop block whose bar is neither G-VER nor the hair trigger,
  off the record and on the last session's id, is refused as "neither" (exact message); at P4's shape it is refused by
  the end-raised pause rule (REF-2's exception).
- **LB-3 and the battery refuter's note on the first form.** Two plants: a step-15 credit and a round-2 credit each
  without its citation's "(".
- **LB-4.** New check STR-PIN-STEP15-TEXTS pins the ordered corrections: no "flagged for ..." phrase anywhere in the
  file; PIN-9's text and site and PIN-10's text credit the reading to the seat by R2 2 (2026-10-10); PIN-7 and PIN-10
  name PIN-46 and PIN-45; PIN-45's text and site comment say the class check runs at every STOP; PIN-45's text states
  the stop->terminal route at the three look shapes and as PI-STOP, CAP and COMPLETE.
- Battery: 448 checks, 461 coverage ids.

## Recorded, not applied

- **FID-3.** PIN-7's list omits PIN-10's own V2 refusal (a STOP with a launch recorded and no end look). Q5 orders
  PIN-45 and PIN-46 named, nothing more. For v1.0's notes.
- **FID-6.** SEAT_ROUTED's PIN-9 entry names C3 and C9(i) only, and the battery labels say "Code's guard for the seat's
  C9(ii)". R2 2 accepted SEAT_ROUTED's paraphrases as listed and said every text naming the seat's C9(ii) over the V2

- **REF-3.** Ids compare by value, and a G-VER STOP's id fields are not shape-checked; no route opens, since each such

---- edit_r2.py (r1 -> r2), verbatim
import sys
p = sys.argv[1]
src = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
def rep(old, new):
    global src
    n = src.count(old)
    assert n == 1, (n, old[:100])
    src = src.replace(old, new)

OTHER = ("any other STOP bar (a truthy stop block that is not an object included) is refused after PIN-8 -- or "
         "earlier, by the end-raised pause rule it selects, where the end look reuses a fired look whose pause was "
         "ruled before the end")
# REF-2: the 'after PIN-8' texts, with the exception
rep('''    # the id off the record passes: PIN-45's limits); any other STOP bar is refused after PIN-8. An end
    # look is fired iff''',
    '''    # the id off the record passes: PIN-45's limits); any other STOP bar (a truthy stop block that is not
    # an object included) is refused after PIN-8 -- or earlier, by the end-raised pause rule it selects,
    # where the end look reuses a fired look whose pause was ruled before the end. An end look is fired iff''')
rep('''        # resume.ended_status); a stop block that is not an object is refused after PIN-8
''', '''        # resume.ended_status); a stop block that is not an object meets no id rule (see above)
''')
rep('''            # the pause rule, by the end's class as recorded (checked above at a STOP); any other STOP bar is
            # refused after PIN-8 whichever rule is chosen
''', '''            # the pause rule, by the end's class as recorded (checked above at a STOP); any other STOP bar, or a
            # stop block that is not an object, selects the end-raised rule here
''')
rep('''"block is recorded, else the terminal block's status (resume.ended_status); a STOP's bar G-VER or "
              "the hair trigger, any other refused after PIN-8 whichever rule is chosen. That class''',
    '''"block is recorded, else the terminal block's status (resume.ended_status); a STOP's bar G-VER or "
              "the hair trigger -- ''' + OTHER + '''. That class''')
# FID-1, FID-4, REF-4(a): the stop->terminal route, CAP and PI-STOP stated apart
rep('''"campaign_look from the session records, which are pick-bearing until V3 (PIN-7's order); a "
              "hair-trigger STOP whose stop block is replaced by a terminal PI-STOP or CAP block reaches VERDICTS "
              "without PIN-8's PI-stop record -- at a silent end look, at a fresh fired one given the seat's "
              "adjudication of its end pause, and at a reused look (a fired one's pause given a ruling) -- with the "
              "shape of that end at n, which ids cannot tell apart (a COMPLETE block too, where every arm's domain "
              "count reaches its recorded floor; with an arm below its floor, V4 STOPs); and a hair-trigger STOP''',
    '''"campaign_look from the session records, which are pick-bearing until V3 (PIN-7's order); a "
              "hair-trigger STOP whose stop block is removed (or left falsy, as resume.ended_status reads its "
              "truthiness) beside a terminal PI-STOP or CAP block reaches VERDICTS without PIN-8's PI-stop record "
              "-- at a silent end look, at a fresh fired one given the seat's adjudication of its end pause, and at "
              "a reused look (a fired one's pause given a ruling): as a PI-STOP it has the ids of the PI's stop at "
              "n, which the writer can make at any n of at least 1 (the fields that differ, campaign.pi_stop and "
              "the stop invocation's provenance entry, V2 does not read); as a CAP it passes at any n, as V2 reads "
              "no hour cap (the writer ends CAP only where state.hour_cap_ms_used reaches the identity's hour_cap: "
              "resume.hour_cap_exceeded); a COMPLETE block passes too where every arm's domain count reaches its "
              "recorded floor (with an arm below its floor, V4 STOPs); and a hair-trigger STOP''')
# FID-2 / REF-5: PIN-10's two new sentences name the class check
rep('''        # G-VER STOP at the first launch) there is no look to carry. A recorded end look's launch count
        # (PIN-46) and a fired one's matching pause (PIN-45) are refused next.''',
    '''        # G-VER STOP at the first launch) there is no look to carry. Next, V2 checks a recorded end look's
        # launch count (PIN-46), a STOP's class by ids and a fired end look's matching pause (PIN-45).''')
rep('''"lock's package); with no launch recorded, 'no look reached'. Where an end look is recorded, "
              "V2 also checks its launch count (PIN-46) and, if it fired, its matching pause (PIN-45). A fired "
              "end look at a STOP is a "''',
    '''"lock's package); with no launch recorded, 'no look reached'. V2 also checks a recorded end "
              "look's launch count (PIN-46), a STOP's class by ids and a fired end look's matching pause "
              "(PIN-45). A fired end look at a STOP is a "''')
# FID-8: labels and the source's name
rep('''                  "at launch 1) and, unedited, analyzed (the hair STOPs with a PI-stop record, the G-VER STOPs with no "
                  "seat input)",''',
    '''                  "at launch 1) and, unedited, analyzed (the hair STOPs with a PI-stop record, the G-VER STOPs with no "
                  "seat input; the G-VER STOP at launch 1 as a NOT-RUN)",''')

    '''    # seat's words (R2 item 3 (a); R2 item 3 (b) and (c); R1 C7's bar; the first paragraph of the step-15 answers'

    '''"C7's bar and the first paragraph of the step-15 answers' Q2, checked against the seat's blocks by Code's "
          "scratch sweep); each "''')

const FINDINGS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  summary: { type: 'string' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['blocker', 'minor', 'note'] }, where: { type: 'string' },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified_ok: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['summary', 'findings', 'verified_ok'] }
const VERDICTS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }

phase('Check')
const chk = await agent(COMMON + `

TASK: an independent check of r3. Work dir ${S2}/chk_r3.
(1) Every "Applied" item of disp_r1.md: is it applied in r3 as described, and does it actually fix the round-1 finding (re-run
the finding's own probe or mutant where one exists)? Every "Recorded, not applied" item: is the stated reason true?
(2) r1->r3 adds no change beyond disp_r1.md (walk every hunk).
(3) Every new or changed text in r3 is true of the code (PIN-45 register and site comment incl. the new CAP/PI-STOP sentence,
the REF-2 exception, the REF-3 clause; PIN-10 register and site sentence; the labels of C3-STOP-CLASS-ORDER,
C3-STOP-CLASS-OTHER-BAR, STR-PIN-STEP15-TEXTS, STR-PIN-V10-TEXTS; the _REQUIRED comment). Verify the CAP and PI-STOP claims
by your own probe (incl. that the writer's PI-STOP records campaign.pi_stop and appends a provenance entry, and that V2 reads
neither; that the writer ends CAP only via resume.hour_cap_exceeded).
(4) The new checks: do their cases mean what the labels say (each case's two defects really present; each fixture as
described)? Write and run at least 10 new mutants against r3's new checks and texts (weakening each conjunct); report each.
(5) Are Code's survivor classifications in equivalents_b.json right? Try to show any 'EQUIVALENT' is not (an input on which
behaviour differs), and any 'ACCEPTED GAP (battery-internal)' changes analyzer behaviour.
Battery must be 448/448 on r3.`, { label: 'check:r3', phase: 'Check', schema: FINDINGS })
phase('Refute')
const ref = await agent(COMMON + `

YOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this check report (verbatim JSON):
${JSON.stringify(chk)}
Work dir ${S2}/ref_chk_r3. For EACH finding: reproduce independently, try to refute it, verdict CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY
with your own evidence paths, and a minimal exact fix if confirmed. Default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-run 3 of
its verified_ok claims; list anything that does not hold, or that it missed, in 'missed'.`, { label: 'refute:r3', phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
return { check: chk, refute: ref }

import sys, ast, textwrap
p = sys.argv[1]
src = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
def rep(old, new):
    global src
    n = src.count(old)
    assert n == 1, (n, old[:100])
    src = src.replace(old, new)

# --- PIN-45 register text (F8a, F6, F2, F8b)
rep('''"the hair trigger -- any other STOP bar (a truthy stop block''', '''"the hair trigger; any other STOP bar (a truthy stop block''')
rep('''"written), after PIN-46's and before PIN-9's branch, so that a deleted pause is never read as no "
              "pause. An unfired''',
    '''"written), after PIN-46's and before PIN-9's branch (and before the pause rule its class selects), so "
              "that a deleted pause is never read as no pause. An unfired''')
rep('''"n, which the writer can make at any n of at least 1 (the fields that differ, campaign.pi_stop and "
              "the stop invocation's provenance entry, V2 does not read); as a CAP it passes at any n, as V2 reads "
              "no hour cap (the writer ends CAP only where state.hour_cap_ms_used reaches the identity's hour_cap: "
              "resume.hour_cap_exceeded); a COMPLETE''',
    '''"n, which the writer can make at any n of at least 1 (the fields that differ, campaign.pi_stop, "
              "the stop invocation's provenance entry and state.n_writes, V2 does not compare); as a CAP it passes "
              "at any n, as V2 compares no hour cap (resume.read_checkpoint checks state.hour_cap_ms_used only "
              "against the sessions' ms, and identity.hour_cap is checked nowhere at V2; the writer ends CAP only "
              "where state.hour_cap_ms_used reaches the identity's hour_cap: resume.hour_cap_exceeded); a COMPLETE''')

# reflow PIN-45's register entry to the file's width (adjacent literals, 14-space indent, <= 118 columns)
start = src.index('    "PIN-45": "Declared at LOCK v1.0')
end = src.index('    "PIN-46": "Declared at LOCK v1.0')
seg = src[start:end]
val = ast.literal_eval("{" + seg.rstrip().rstrip(",") + "}")["PIN-45"]
words = val.split(" ")
lines, cur = [], ""
first_pre, cont_pre = '    "PIN-45": "', '              "'
width = 118
for w in words:
    pre = first_pre if not lines else cont_pre
    cand = (cur + " " + w) if cur else w
    body = cand.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"')
    if len(pre) + len(body) + 3 > width and cur:
        lines.append(cur + " ")
        cur = w
    else:
        cur = cand
lines.append(cur)
out = []
for i, l in enumerate(lines):
    pre = first_pre if i == 0 else cont_pre
    out.append(pre + l.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"')
new_seg = "\n".join(out) + ",\n"
assert ast.literal_eval("{" + new_seg.rstrip().rstrip(",") + "}")["PIN-45"] == val
src = src[:start] + new_seg + src[end:]

# --- PIN-45 site comment (F6)
rep('''    # writes ints and strs. The pause rule is chosen by the end's class as recorded. Checked after
    # PIN-46's and before PIN-9's branch, so a deleted pause is never read as no pause.''',
    '''    # writes ints and strs. The pause rule is chosen by the end's class as recorded. Checked after
    # PIN-46's and before PIN-9's branch (and before the pause rule its class selects), so a deleted pause
    # is never read as no pause.''')

---- THE WORKFLOW SCRIPT (step15b-table-draft-wf_6c9b14df-0db.js; run once and stopped, then relaunched fresh as run wf_cd23f6e6-703), verbatim
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-table-draft',
  description: 'Draft the sentence table of record from the placed preprint: three independent classifiers, adjudication, two adversarial critics',
  phases: [{ title: 'Classify' }, { title: 'Adjudicate' }, { title: 'Critique' }],
}
const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. You help Code build the DRAFT sentence table of record for PROPOSAL-m's Q-F0 posting (repo ${R}). Read IN FULL,
first: the spec ${S2}/table/spec.md (the rules are Code's readings; apply them exactly; where a rule does not decide a
unit, say so); the preprint of record ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (patch v8; do not edit it); its units
${S2}/table/sentences_v8.txt (one per line: "line.sentence<TAB>[section]<TAB>text"). Background you may read: the
proposal's posting rule ${R}/PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md line 349 and Step 2's labels at line 338; the seat's
step-15 answers ${R}/results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md (Q3 on §8.1's kinds).
HARD RULES: read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/, nothing under results/raw/ other than the answers file
above; modify nothing in ${R}; no git. Outcome-blind: nothing about any campaign result exists or is needed.
Refer to units by "line.sentence" exactly as sentences_v8.txt numbers them.`
const ROW = { type: 'object', properties: {
  unit: { type: 'string' }, null_cells: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string', enum: ['e2a', 'f1', 'e2b', 'weight', 'S1', 'olmo-base', 'olmo-sft', 'olmo-dpo', 'olmo-instruct'] } },
  thesis: { type: ['string', 'null'], enum: ['main', 'fallback-1', 'fallback-2', null] }, dose_note: { type: 'boolean' },
  dose_note_span: { type: ['string', 'null'] }, kinds: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
  boundary: { type: 'boolean' }, rationale: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['unit', 'null_cells', 'thesis', 'dose_note', 'dose_note_span', 'kinds', 'boundary', 'rationale'] }
const CLASSIFY = { type: 'object', properties: {
  rows: { type: 'array', items: ROW },
  considered_excluded: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { unit: { type: 'string' }, why: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['unit', 'why'] } },
  spec_gaps: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['rows', 'considered_excluded', 'spec_gaps'] }

const METHODS = [
  { key: 'linear', how: 'Read the preprint front to back, unit by unit, from line 2 to line 456 (skipping the excluded regions), and classify every unit that qualifies under the spec\'s inclusion rule.' },
  { key: 'by-cell', how: 'Work cell by cell: for each of the nine cells in turn (e2a, f1, e2b, weight, S1, olmo-base, olmo-sft, olmo-dpo, olmo-instruct), search the WHOLE text (grep the units for every term that names the cell or its result: e.g. transcript, self-administration, present-tense, state live, weight, adapter, text channel, recognition, natural reinforcer, task propert, corruption, OLMo, SFT, DPO, instruct, base, stage, lineage, null, Answered, choice, move, act on, coupling, consumer, reinforcer, want, seek) and decide every unit that touches it; then sweep theses (functional for the character, not for the network, story, used/valenced, not acted on, consumer, reinforcer, marked/expressed/remembered); then the dose note and the kind rows. Merge into one row per unit.' },
  { key: 'by-clause', how: 'Work clause by clause, starting from the END of the paper and moving backwards: first (b) — every unit that states or paraphrases a thesis (main, fallback-1, fallback-2) — then (a) — every unit asserting a null by the spec\'s kinds 1-3 — then (c) and the kind rows. Merge into one row per unit.' },
]

phase('Classify')
const cls = await parallel(METHODS.map(m => () => agent(COMMON + `

TASK (${m.key}): ${m.how}
Return one row per qualifying unit (fields per the schema; null_cells sorted in the order e2a, f1, e2b, weight, S1,
olmo-base, olmo-sft, olmo-dpo, olmo-instruct; kinds from the spec's vocabulary only; boundary true where the spec's
"ambiguous scope" rule or any judgement call decided the row; a one-to-three sentence rationale quoting the words that
decide it). In considered_excluded list every unit you weighed and left out, with why (especially figure reports,
labels of concluded cells, design and §8 rule sentences). In spec_gaps list any case the spec does not decide.`,
  { label: 'classify:' + m.key, phase: 'Classify', schema: CLASSIFY })))

// deterministic merge
const by = {}
cls.forEach((c, i) => { if (!c) return; for (const r of c.rows) { (by[r.unit] = by[r.unit] || {})[METHODS[i].key] = r } })
const norm = r => JSON.stringify([r.null_cells.slice().sort(), r.thesis, r.dose_note, r.dose_note_span, r.kinds.slice().sort()])
const agree = [], disagree = []
for (const [u, m] of Object.entries(by)) {
  const ks = Object.keys(m)
  const same = ks.length === METHODS.length && new Set(ks.map(k => norm(m[k]))).size === 1
  ;(same ? agree : disagree).push({ unit: u, by: m })
}
log(`classifiers: ${cls.map(c => c ? c.rows.length : 'null').join(' / ')} rows; ${agree.length} units agreed, ${disagree.length} to adjudicate`)

phase('Adjudicate')
const adj = await agent(COMMON + `

TASK: ADJUDICATE. Three independent classifiers produced rows. These units have identical rows from all three (accepted
unless you find one clearly wrong under the spec; list any such in 'overrides'):
${JSON.stringify(agree.map(a => ({ unit: a.unit, row: Object.values(a.by)[0] })))}
These units differ between the classifiers, or were found by fewer than three (decide each under the spec; a unit only
one classifier found may still be right — re-read it):
${JSON.stringify(disagree)}
Their excluded lists and spec gaps: ${JSON.stringify(cls.map((c, i) => c ? { method: METHODS[i].key, considered_excluded: c.considered_excluded, spec_gaps: c.spec_gaps } : null))}
Return the FINAL rows (every unit that belongs in the table, agreed ones included, one row each), with boundary true
where any judgement decided the row, and a rationale. In considered_excluded list the units you decided to leave out
that any classifier had included, with why. In spec_gaps list the gaps that changed a decision.`,
  { label: 'adjudicate', phase: 'Adjudicate', schema: CLASSIFY })

phase('Critique')
const CRIT = { type: 'object', properties: {
  missing: { type: 'array', items: ROW },
  challenged: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { unit: { type: 'string' }, problem: { type: 'string' }, proposed: ROW }, required: ['unit', 'problem'] } },
  notes: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['missing', 'challenged', 'notes'] }
const crit = await parallel([
  () => agent(COMMON + `

TASK: COMPLETENESS CRITIC. The adjudicated table (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(adj ? adj.rows : [])}
Go through EVERY unit of sentences_v8.txt outside the excluded regions that is NOT in the table and decide whether the
spec's inclusion rule requires it. Be adversarial: the costly error is an omitted unit that asserts a null on a cell
(especially e2b, the weight channel or the OLMo base row, which are never Answered) or a thesis. Return each unit that
must be added as a full row in 'missing' (with rationale quoting the decisive words); put near-misses in 'notes'.`,
    { label: 'critic:completeness', phase: 'Critique', schema: CRIT }),
  () => agent(COMMON + `

TASK: CLASSIFICATION REFUTER. The adjudicated table (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(adj ? adj.rows : [])}
For EVERY row, try to refute its null_cells (a cell included that the unit's claim does not cover, or a covered cell
missing), its thesis (wrong level, or a thesis missing), its dose note and span (verbatim? the right extent?), and its
kinds (against the spec's kinds map). Apply the spec exactly; where the spec itself seems wrong for a unit, say so in
'notes' rather than deviating. Return each row you would change in 'challenged' with the problem and the proposed row.`,
    { label: 'critic:classification', phase: 'Critique', schema: CRIT }),
])
return { classifiers: cls, merge: { agree: agree.length, disagree: disagree.map(d => d.unit) }, adjudicated: adj, critics: crit }

 "spec_gaps": [
  "General conclusions vs never-Answered cells. Spec (a)'s general-conclusion list ('e2b where the unit includes the state-live read'; olmo-base never named) can collide with its ambiguous-scope rule ('include it, and mark the row a boundary case'). Several units' literal scope reaches every felt cell: 5.1 and 13.15 'when its consequences must be learned', 232.2 'when the only route is experience', 240.2 'in our data none does', 194.4 'absent everywhere', 254.2 'Every null', 25.4 'one full post-training lineage'. I applied the general-conclusion list, because §3.4 (91.1) says a concluded cell 'licenses nothing about the state', so the paper's nulls do not reach e2b or base. Reading e2b or base into these units would make every fallback-1 row fire in all 1,080 tuples, so the Fallback-1 thesis could never be posted. The spec does not say which of its two rules governs; the seat may want to rule.",
  "The main-thesis formula and rule (a). The spec does not say whether 'not for the network' / 'not what the system is for' is itself a kind-2 null. Main rows without an explicit phrase saying choice is absent (3.1, 230.1, 234.1, 234.5, 234.6, 258.4) have null_cells []. This does not change firing at v1, since main fires in all 1,080 tuples, but it matters at v2 if Main becomes reachable.",
  "Half-thesis units. Spec (b) defines main paraphrases of the whole used/valenced split but not of one half. 234.2, the used half ('voice the character correctly'), is excluded because it is not above any ceiling. 234.4, the valenced half ('consulted never'), is given main rather than fallback-1. Both are judgements.",
  "Thesis level of single-cell or single-lineage nulls.   "Design and §8 sentences that presuppose nulls.  "spec_gaps": [
  "Which spec rule governs e2b. Spec (a)'s general list includes e2b only 'where the unit includes the state-live read'. I read it as: the unit names that read, or ranges over data that contain it. On that reading, by-cell's e2b is struck from 5.1, 13.15, 123.1, 168.1, 194.4, 232.2, 232.3, 234.4, 236.1, 236.2 and 240.3. e2b is kept on 220.1 ('leakage certification') and 262.5 (the spec's mapping of 'self-administration nulls'), and added on 240.2 ('in our data') and 250.3 ('the data in hand'). The seat may want to rule once for all these rows.",
  "Which spec rule governs olmo-base. The general list leaves out base without stating a condition, and the ambiguity rule says to include and mark boundary. I kept the general list for bare general conclusions. I applied the ambiguity rule where the unit, or the paragraph it continues, names the OLMo lineage or the paper's data as the scope of the null claim: 25.4 'one full post-training lineage'; 232.1–232.3 'across two families and one lineage' (232.2's sibling clauses say 'at the pretrained base' and 'at every stage'); 240.6 'on these lineages'; 240.2 'in our data'; 250.3 'the data in hand'; 220.1 'This paper'; and 262.5 'at each stage', where all three classifiers agreed. These are findings against 468.1 ('the OLMo base row carry no null anywhere'). The checks 468.1 cites cover the abstract, contribution 2, §5.5, §8.1 and §7.3, not §1, §7.1, §7.2, §7.4 or §7.6. The seat may strike base on any of these rows.",
  "Whether 'returns the same figure' asserts.   "Null cells on pure thesis formulas. The spec does not say whether 'not for the network' or 'not what the system is for' is itself a kind-2 null. HOW TO RUN. ${S2}/runst15b.sh <src.py> <run-id> [${S2}/mut15.py <mutant-id>] (env MUTANTS = JSON list of {id, old, new} or
{id, edits}; ALWAYS set PLACEHOLDER=${S2}/table/placeholder.r6.json for r6, else ENUM-PLACEHOLDER-FILE fails on HEAD's file;
set your own OUTDIR and WDIR under ${S2}/<your-dir>/). It clones HEAD (data/m/ckpt/*.json removed unread), copies the src
over scripts/m_analyze.py, applies at most one mutant, runs the battery (~27 s; 448 checks, 461 ids). The table builder runs as:
cd ${R} && PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=${S2.replace('step15b', 'step12')}/mt3/net jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python -I ${S2}/table/build_table.py <analyzer.py> <rows.json> ${S2}/table/sentences_v8.json ${R} <out.json> <report.txt>
(write outputs under your dir only).

HARD RULES. Never open, list or hash anything under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt;

const FINDINGS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  summary: { type: 'string' },
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['blocker', 'minor', 'note'] }, where: { type: 'string' },
    finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  verified_ok: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
  mutants: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, intent: { type: 'string' },
    outcome: { type: 'string' }, detail: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'outcome'] } } },
  required: ['summary', 'findings', 'verified_ok'] }
const VERDICTS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', prompt: `LENS: WHOLE-ROUND FIDELITY. Work dir ${S2}/fin_fid.
(1) Walk every hunk of r6.diff: each is ordered by the answers (Q2, Q5, the scope sentence, Q3, the KINDS_VOCAB need), or
is a disposition in disp_r1/disp_r3, or a consequence of one; name any that is none of these.
(2) PIN-17 re-verified against the placed v8 yourself: §8.1 (v8 lines 268-280) holds A0, A0-OLMo (SFT/DPO/instruct), A1, A2,
A3; §8.2 is the Tagliabue arms; s81_text's four clauses and S81_CONTROLS match the v1 coordinates; m:343's tokens; Step 7
'open' at v1. Is r6's PIN-17 text, site comment and SEAT_ROUTED line accurate, in the seat's words where it quotes them, and
dated right? Is anything else in r6 still saying A1 belongs to §8.2, or otherwise stale against v8 (grep §8.2, §8.1, 8.2)?
(3) The new kind: is 's8.1-a1-a3' the need Q3 names, and is adding it (and regenerating the placeholder whose hash LOCK v0.1
names) the "KINDS_VOCAB need" Q6 anticipates, or does it move anything the order did not license? Check the placeholder
diff is exactly the one vocabulary line, and that nothing else in the repo (grep the tracked tree outside data/m/ckpt) reads
the placeholder's hash or the vocabulary in a way the move breaks.
(4) Every text r6 adds or changes over the whole round is true of the code and the sources (sample the PIN-45, PIN-10, PIN-9,
PIN-7 texts again against r6's code; quotations verbatim; dates; question numbers).
(5) The spec's kinds map (spec.md 'Kinds'): are the v1.1->v8 counterparts right (read ${R}/PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md §7 and its
header note), and are 's7.2-sceptic', 's7.7-penultimate' and 'tab2-row' truly without a counterpart in v8?` },
  { key: 'battery', prompt: `LENS: BATTERY, PLACEHOLDER AND TABLE DRAFT. Work dir ${S2}/fin_bat.
(1) r6 passes 448/448 with PLACEHOLDER set; without it, which check fails and why (expected: ENUM-PLACEHOLDER-FILE only)?
Regenerate the placeholder yourself from r6's placeholder_table() with resume.json_text(indent=1) and confirm it is byte-equal
to ${S2}/table/placeholder.r6.json.
(2) Mutants of r6's new changes (the kind's spelling, position and removal; PIN-17's texts; SEAT_ROUTED) in mut15.py form,
run with PLACEHOLDER set; report each (KILLED by which check / SURVIVED / EQUIVALENT with the reason). Which text changes of
the round does no check hold (list them; they are review-only)?
(3) The table draft: re-run build_table.py yourself on draft_rows_v1.json and confirm the draft and table_problems [] and the
fire counts. Then probe table_problems against the draft for anything the schema lets through that it should not (a row
whose text occurs in the preprint but not on its line, a unit split across lines, ids, the of-record preprint record), and
confirm the draft exercises no schema gap and needs no further KINDS_VOCAB entry (every kind the spec maps is in r6's
vocabulary; any place the spec names that would need a kind it lacks?).
(4) Run the segmenter's checks: every unit's text lies on its line; ordinals strictly increase; no unit straddles two
sentences in a way that would make a row's text cover two claims with different cells (sample the 56 rows' units).` },
]
const results = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + '\n\n' + l.prompt, { label: 'final:' + l.key, phase: 'Review', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens. Work dir ${S2}/fin_ref_${l.key}.
Its report (verbatim JSON):\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\n
For EACH finding: reproduce independently, try to refute it, verdict CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your own evidence, and
a minimal exact fix if confirmed. Default to REFUTED when you cannot reproduce. Re-run 3 of its verified_ok claims and 3 of its
mutant outcomes; report anything that does not hold, or that it missed, in 'missed'.`, { label: 'refute:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
    .then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null,
)
return results
